  # 1. 原始問題                                                                
  question = """情資蒐集指導如下，時間範圍2023年：                             
  俄烏戰爭使歐洲能源格局重組，天然氣供應改由美國、中東與北海替代，推升能源轉   
  型速度；歐盟加大國防與科技自主投入，帶動軍工、資安、無人機、通訊設備等產業   
  需求；同時，供應鏈外移加速東歐與新興市場的基建、製造布局。戰爭仍具不確定性   
  ，但中長期投資機會集中在能源替代、戰略科技、自主供應鏈與重建經濟等主題。     
  情資蒐集請依下列方向進行：宏觀組追蹤能源價格、天然氣儲備、水陸運輸成本及歐   
  盟產業補貼政策；產業組聚焦軍工供應鏈、資安技術、半導體自主化、再生能源與電   
  網投資；政策組研判制裁措施、軍援規模與外交動向對市場的影響；企業組關注歐洲   
  與美國國防企業訂單量、能源公司資本支出規劃與重建相關標案；資料情資組負責戰   
  場態勢、社群輿情與供應鏈異常的早期指標。所有情資須以投資風險與預期報酬為軸   
  心，形成可支撐策略布局的分析摘要。"""                                        
                                                                               
  # 2. 從問題中抽取核心關鍵詞 (不含時間字串)                                   
  core_terms = [                                                               
      "俄烏戰爭",                                                              
      "歐洲",                                                                  
      "能源",                                                                  
      "軍工",                                                                  
      "資安",                                                                  
      "半導體自主化",                                                          
      "再生能源",                                                              
      "供應鏈"                                                                 
  ]                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 3. 組成 ES 查詢字串                                                        
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  # 4. 時間範圍 (2023 年)                                                      
  date_from = "2023-01-01"                                                     
  date_to   = "2023-12-31"                                                     
                                                                               
  # 5. 呼叫 ES 搜尋                                                            
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint="news_test"                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 6. 取得 evidence snippets                                                  
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 7. 統計彙總                                                                
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
                                                                               
  doc_count      = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                             
  snippet_count  = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                         
  active_days    = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                           
  day_counts     = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                           
  month_counts   = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                         
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 8. 依時間範圍過濾統計結果                                                  
  if date_from or date_to:                                                     
      start_date = date_from                                                   
      end_date   = date_to                                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 day_counts                                                        
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if start_date and d < start_date:                                    
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and d > end_date:                                        
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # 過濾 month_counts（保留完全落在範圍內的月份）                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():  # m 形如 "2023-05"                  
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last  = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"                                   
          if start_date and m_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and m_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 quarter_counts（形如 "2023-Q2"）                                  
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]  # '1'..'4'                                             
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:                                                                
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
                                                                               
          if start_date and q_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and q_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 9. 取得 ES 回傳的 query_meta（若無則給空 dict）                            
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  # 10. 組成 query_meta                                                        
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 11. 組成最終 payload                                                       
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 12. 回傳 payload                                                           
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })

  # 1. 原始問題                                                                
  question = """情資蒐集指導如下，時間範圍2023年：                             
  俄烏戰爭使歐洲能源格局重組，天然氣供應改由美國、中東與北海替代，推升能源轉   
  型速度；歐盟加大國防與科技自主投入，帶動軍工、資安、無人機、通訊設備等產業   
  需求；同時，供應鏈外移加速東歐與新興市場的基建、製造布局。戰爭仍具不確定性   
  ，但中長期投資機會集中在能源替代、戰略科技、自主供應鏈與重建經濟等主題。     
  情資蒐集請依下列方向進行：宏觀組追蹤能源價格、天然氣儲備、水陸運輸成本及歐   
  盟產業補貼政策；產業組聚焦軍工供應鏈、資安技術、半導體自主化、再生能源與電   
  網投資；政策組研判制裁措施、軍援規模與外交動向對市場的影響；企業組關注歐洲   
  與美國國防企業訂單量、能源公司資本支出規劃與重建相關標案；資料情資組負責戰   
  場態勢、社群輿情與供應鏈異常的早期指標。所有情資須以投資風險與預期報酬為軸   
  心，形成可支撐策略布局的分析摘要。"""                                        
                                                                               
  # 2. 從問題中抽取核心關鍵詞 (不含時間字串)                                   
  core_terms = [                                                               
      "俄烏戰爭",                                                              
      "歐洲",                                                                  
      "能源",                                                                  
      "軍工",                                                                  
      "資安",                                                                  
      "半導體自主化",                                                          
      "再生能源",                                                              
      "供應鏈"                                                                 
  ]                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 3. 組成 ES 查詢字串                                                        
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  # 4. 時間範圍 (2023 年)                                                      
  date_from = "2023-01-01"                                                     
  date_to   = "2023-12-31"                                                     
                                                                               
  # 5. 呼叫 ES 搜尋                                                            
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint="news_test"                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 6. 取得 evidence snippets                                                  
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 7. 統計彙總                                                                
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
                                                                               
  doc_count      = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                             
  snippet_count  = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                         
  active_days    = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                           
  day_counts     = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                           
  month_counts   = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                         
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 8. 依時間範圍過濾統計結果                                                  
  if date_from or date_to:                                                     
      start_date = date_from                                                   
      end_date   = date_to                                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 day_counts                                                        
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if start_date and d < start_date:                                    
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and d > end_date:                                        
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # 過濾 month_counts（保留完全落在範圍內的月份）                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():  # m 形如 "2023-05"                  
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last  = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"                                   
          if start_date and m_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and m_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 quarter_counts（形如 "2023-Q2"）                                  
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]  # '1'..'4'                                             
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:                                                                
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
                                                                               
          if start_date and q_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and q_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 9. 取得 ES 回傳的 query_meta（若無則給空 dict）                            
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  # 10. 組成 query_meta                                                        
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 11. 組成最終 payload                                                       
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 12. 回傳 payload                                                           
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })

  需求；同時，供應鏈外移加速東歐與新興市場的基建、製造布局。戰爭仍具不確定性   
  ，但中長期投資機會集中在能源替代、戰略科技、自主供應鏈與重建經濟等主題。     
  情資蒐集請依下列方向進行：宏觀組追蹤能源價格、天然氣儲備、水陸運輸成本及歐   
  盟產業補貼政策；產業組聚焦軍工供應鏈、資安技術、半導體自主化、再生能源與電   
  網投資；政策組研判制裁措施、軍援規模與外交動向對市場的影響；企業組關注歐洲   
  與美國國防企業訂單量、能源公司資本支出規劃與重建相關標案；資料情資組負責戰   
  場態勢、社群輿情與供應鏈異常的早期指標。所有情資須以投資風險與預期報酬為軸   
  心，形成可支撐策略布局的分析摘要。"""                                        
                                                                               
  # 2. 從問題中抽取核心關鍵詞 (不含時間字串)                                   
  core_terms = [                                                               
      "俄烏戰爭",                                                              
      "歐洲",                                                                  
      "能源",                                                                  
      "軍工",                                                                  
      "資安",                                                                  
      "半導體自主化",                                                          
      "再生能源",                                                              
      "供應鏈"                                                                 
  ]                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 3. 組成 ES 查詢字串                                                        
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  # 4. 時間範圍 (2023 年)                                                      
  date_from = "2023-01-01"                                                     
  date_to   = "2023-12-31"                                                     
                                                                               
  # 5. 呼叫 ES 搜尋                                                            
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint="news_test"                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 6. 取得 evidence snippets                                                  
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 7. 統計彙總                                                                
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
                                                                               
  doc_count      = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                             
  snippet_count  = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                         
  active_days    = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                           
  day_counts     = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                           
  month_counts   = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                         
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 8. 依時間範圍過濾統計結果                                                  
  if date_from or date_to:                                                     
      start_date = date_from                                                   
      end_date   = date_to                                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 day_counts                                                        
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if start_date and d < start_date:                                    
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and d > end_date:                                        
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # 過濾 month_counts（保留完全落在範圍內的月份）                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():  # m 形如 "2023-05"                  
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last  = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"                                   
          if start_date and m_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and m_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 quarter_counts（形如 "2023-Q2"）                                  
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]  # '1'..'4'                                             
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:                                                                
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
                                                                               
          if start_date and q_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and q_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 9. 取得 ES 回傳的 query_meta（若無則給空 dict）                            
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  # 10. 組成 query_meta                                                        
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 11. 組成最終 payload                                                       
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 12. 回傳 payload                                                           
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })
